### 3.2 Detailed Technical Breakdown of Critical & High Findings (`W5-01` → `W5-14`)

#### `W5-01` — `[CRITICAL] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `PutawayService.MoveAsync` Ignores Receipt-Declared Lot/Serial Identities at `RECEIVING`, Causing Cross-Receipt Lot/Serial Contamination, False `409 INV_STOCK_LOCKED`, and Missing `UPDLOCK` on `InventoryStocks`
- **Location:** [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PutawayService.cs:430-534`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PutawayService.cs#L430-L534), [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ReceiptInventoryIdentityService.cs:125-236`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ReceiptInventoryIdentityService.cs#L125-L236)
- **Root Cause:**
  1. When an `ImportReceipt` is posted, `ReceiptInventoryIdentityService.ApplyIdentitiesOnPostAsync` creates `InventoryStock` buckets at the `RECEIVING` staging location (`SourceLocationId`) with the specific `LotId` and `SerialId` declared in `ImportReceiptInventoryIdentities` for each `ImportReceiptDetail` (`ReceiptLineId`).
  2. When `PutawayService.MoveAsync` (`#L430-L439`) moves `request.Quantity` for a `PutawayTaskItem` (`item.ReceiptLineId`), it never filters by `ImportReceiptInventoryIdentities` of `item.ReceiptLineId`. Instead, it queries **all** `InventoryStocks` at `item.SourceLocationId` ordered by `LotId, SerialId, Id`.
  3. Furthermore, `MoveAsync` acquires `UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK` on `WarehouseLocations` (`#L550`), **not** on `InventoryStocks` (`#L430`, `#L487`).
- **Reproduction Steps:**
  1. Post Receipt 1 (`ReceiptLineId = 101`) with 10 units of Product P (`LotId = 10`) into `RECEIVING`.
  2. Post Receipt 2 (`ReceiptLineId = 102`) with 10 units of Product P (`LotId = 20`) into `RECEIVING`.
  3. Execute `PutawayService.MoveAsync` for `PutawayTask 2` (`ReceiptLineId = 102`) first: `OrderBy(x => x.LotId)` selects `LotId = 10` (Receipt 1's lot!) and moves it under `Receipt2.Id`. If `LotId = 10` has an active `InventoryLock`, `PutawayTask 2` fails with `409 INV_STOCK_LOCKED` even though Receipt 2's `LotId = 20` is unlocked.
- **Recommended Fix:** In `PutawayService.MoveAsync`, load `_db.ImportReceiptInventoryIdentities.Where(x => x.ImportReceiptDetailId == item.ReceiptLineId)` and constrain `sourceBuckets` to the `LotId`/`SerialId` set declared for that receipt line (when identities exist), and acquire `UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK, HOLDLOCK` on the source/destination `InventoryStocks` rows.

#### `W5-02` — `[CRITICAL] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `PickingService.ResolveAlternativeLocationAsync` Steals Other Orders' Uncommitted `ReservedQuantity` at Candidate Bins and Leaks Phantom `ReservedQuantity` at the Short-Picked Source Bin
- **Location:** [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PickingService.cs:394-518`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PickingService.cs#L394-L518)
- **Root Cause:**
  1. At candidate bin B (`#L421-L425`), `uncommittedReserved = Math.Max(0m, candidate.Stock.ReservedQuantity - candidate.Committed)` is treated as available to take (`takeFromReserved`). However, `StockReservationService.TryReserveAsync` reserves specific bins, so `uncommittedReserved` at Bin B belongs to **another order's unallocated `StockReservation`**, not the current order!
  2. Worse, `#L446-L453` only increments `totalShiftedFromSource += takeFromUnreserved`. When `takeFromReserved > 0` at Bin B, the short-picked source bin A's `ReservedQuantity` (`#L489-L518`) is **not** decremented by `takeFromReserved`, leaving permanent phantom `ReservedQuantity` at Bin A after dispatch.
- **Recommended Fix:** At alternative candidate bins (`candidate.Stock.Id != originalSourceBucketId`), only take from `unreservedAvailable = Math.Max(0m, candidate.Stock.Quantity - candidate.Stock.ReservedQuantity)`, increment `candidate.Stock.ReservedQuantity += take`, and decrement the original short-picked source bin's `ReservedQuantity` by the full shifted quantity.

#### `W5-03` — `[CRITICAL] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `WarehouseService.DeleteWarehouseAsync` Always Crashes with Unhandled `500 DbUpdateException` on Every Created Warehouse
- **Location:** [`ERP.Application/Services/WarehouseService.cs:73-131`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/WarehouseService.cs#L73-L131), [`ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/WarehouseRepository.cs:24-33`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/WarehouseRepository.cs#L24-L33), [`ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/PutawayConfiguration.cs:16`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/PutawayConfiguration.cs#L16)
- **Root Cause:** `CreateWarehouseAsync` seeds mandatory system-managed `WarehouseLocations` (`RECEIVING`, `LEGACY`, `DAMAGED`). `FK_WarehouseLocations_Warehouses_WarehouseId` uses `DeleteBehavior.Restrict`. `WarehouseRepository.HasTransactionsAsync(id)` only checks `InventoryTransactions` and `InventoryStocks (Quantity > 0)`. Calling `DeleteWarehouseAsync` on a brand-new warehouse immediately throws a SQL FK constraint violation (`500 DbUpdateException`).
- **Recommended Fix:** Expand `WarehouseRepository.HasTransactionsAsync` to check all dependent operational/configuration tables (non-system `WarehouseLocations`, any receipts/orders/transfers/stocktakes/locks/docks/calendars/user assignments), and when a warehouse has zero operational references, remove its seeded system-managed `WarehouseLocations` (`RECEIVING`, `LEGACY`, `DAMAGED`) and zero-quantity `InventoryStocks` inside the delete transaction before deleting the `Warehouse`.

#### `W5-04` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** Short-Pick `Backorder` Can Never Be Reallocated via `ReallocateBackorderAsync` (`409 BACKORDER_EXECUTION_STARTED`), and `SupervisorOverride` Conflates Cancellation / Blocks `ExportReceipt` Dispatch
- **Location:** [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/SalesOrderBackorderService.cs:313-321`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/SalesOrderBackorderService.cs#L313-L321), [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PickingService.cs:318-323,615-641`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PickingService.cs#L615-L641)
- **Root Cause:**
  1. `ReallocateBackorderAsync` (`#L313-L321`) throws `409 BACKORDER_EXECUTION_STARTED` whenever any non-cancelled `PickingTask` for `so.Id` has `Status is not Open and not Assigned`. Because short-pick backorders are only created after picking is `InProgress`/`Shortage`/`Resolved`/`Completed`, **100% of short-pick backorders are blocked from reallocation**.
  2. In `PickingService.ResolveReleasedRemainderAsync` (`#L615-L641`), the `else` branch increments `soLine.CancelledQuantity` for both `CancelRemainder` and `SupervisorOverride`, and `EnsureSourceReadyAsync` (`#L318-L323`) blocks `ExportReceipt` dispatch when `pickedQty < detail.Quantity` even after `SupervisorOverride`.
- **Recommended Fix:** Allow `ReallocateBackorderAsync` to allocate open backorder quantities and generate a supplemental `PickingTask` (or attach to an open task) when prior picking tasks are already `Resolved`/`Completed`; handle `SupervisorOverride` distinctly from `CancelRemainder` and allow `ExportReceipt` readiness when shortage lines have an approved resolution.

#### `W5-05` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `ImportReceiptService.ApproveImportReceiptAsync` Transitions Over-Tolerance PO Receipts into an Unrecoverable `ReadyToPost` State
- **Location:** [`ERP.Application/Services/ImportReceiptService.cs:292-348,487,579`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/ImportReceiptService.cs#L292-L348), [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ReceiptInboundPlanningIntegration.cs:143-173`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ReceiptInboundPlanningIntegration.cs#L143-L173)
- **Root Cause:** `ApproveImportReceiptAsync` transitions a receipt to `ReceiptStatus.ReadyToPost` without calling `_inboundPlanning.ValidatePostAsync(receipt)`. When `PostAsync` subsequently fails PO cumulative over-receipt tolerance (`409`), the receipt is stuck in `ReadyToPost` where neither `CancelAsync` (only allows `Draft`) nor `ApprovalWorkflowService.RejectAsync` can recover it.
- **Recommended Fix:** Call `await _inboundPlanning.ValidatePostAsync(receipt, cancellationToken)` inside `ApproveImportReceiptAsync` before transitioning to `ReadyToPost`, and allow `CancelAsync` (or supervisor rejection) to cancel unposted receipts while restoring linked ASN status.

#### `W5-06` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `ApprovalWorkflowService.RejectAsync` Fails with `409 ConcurrencyException` on `QcCompleted` `ImportReceipt`s, and `ApprovalsController.SlaSummary`/`EscalateOverdue` Truncate at `PageSize = 100`
- **Location:** [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ApprovalWorkflowService.cs:54,201,228,260`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ApprovalWorkflowService.cs#L228), [`ERP.Api/Controllers/ApprovalsController.cs:36,87-96`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Controllers/ApprovalsController.cs#L87-L96)
- **Root Cause:**
  1. `GetQueueAsync` and `GetDetailAsync` include `ImportReceipt`s in `ReceiptStatus.Received` or `ReceiptStatus.QcCompleted` with `CanReject = true`, but `RejectAsync` (`#L228`) only matches `x.Status == ReceiptStatus.Received`, throwing `ConcurrencyException` (`409`) when rejecting a `QcCompleted` receipt.
  2. `ApprovalsController.SlaSummary` and `EscalateOverdue` query `PageSize = 100`, truncating SLA counts when `TotalRecords > 100`.
- **Recommended Fix:** Update `ApprovalWorkflowService.RejectReceiptAsync` for `ImportReceipt` to accept both `ReceiptStatus.Received` and `ReceiptStatus.QcCompleted` (and `ReadyToPost`), and compute `SlaSummary` / `EscalateOverdue` across all pending items.

#### `W5-07` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `StocktakeService` Collapses Lot/Serial Buckets by `(ProductId, LocationId)` and Leaks `SystemQuantity` on Blind Stocktakes (`[BLIND]`)
- **Location:** [`ERP.Application/Services/StocktakeService.cs:100-108,212-300,422-466`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/StocktakeService.cs#L212-L300), [`ERP.Infrastructure/Queries/StocktakeQueryService.cs:105-117`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Queries/StocktakeQueryService.cs#L105-L117)
- **Root Cause:**
  1. `ApproveStocktakeAsync` inserts `SerialId = null` / `LotId = null` rows on positive variance for serial/lot-tracked products (`#L225-240`) and zeroes out `InventoryStock.Quantity` on negative variance without updating `InventorySerial.Status` (`#L269-299`).
  2. `StocktakeService.MapToDto` (`#L422-466`) never masks `SystemQuantity`/`DifferenceQuantity` for `[BLIND]` stocktakes, and `StocktakeQueryService.GetStocktakeByIdAsync` (`#L105-117`) unmasks `SystemQuantity` while still in `Draft` as soon as `detail.ActualQuantity.HasValue` is true.
- **Recommended Fix:** Keep `SystemQuantity` and `DifferenceQuantity` masked (`0` or `null` in blind DTOs) in `StocktakeService.MapToDto`, `StocktakeQueryService`, and `ApprovalWorkflowService` for the entire `StocktakeStatus.Draft` lifecycle; in `ApproveStocktakeAsync`, synchronize `InventorySerial.Status` on negative variance and require/preserve valid `LotId`/`SerialId` invariants.

#### `W5-08` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `StockTransferService.ReceiveAsync` Silently Drops `DamagedQuantity` From Both `InventoryStocks` and `InventoryTransactions`
- **Location:** [`ERP.Infrastructure/Services/StockTransferService.cs:257-269`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/StockTransferService.cs#L257-L269)
- **Root Cause:** When receiving an inter-warehouse transfer with `DamagedQuantity > 0`, `ReceiveAsync` only posts `ReceivedQuantity` to `InventoryStocks` (`Available`) and `InventoryTransactions` (`TransferIn`). The `DamagedQuantity` units deducted from the source warehouse on dispatch vanish from physical inventory and the ledger at the destination warehouse.
- **Recommended Fix:** When `receiveLine.DamagedQuantity > 0`, increment or create a destination `InventoryStock` bucket with `InventoryStatus.Damaged` at a dedicated non-pickable `DAMAGED` location (`IsPickable = false`) and record a corresponding `TransferIn` ledger entry for the damaged quantity.

#### `W5-09` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `ProductService.UpdateProductAsync` & `DeleteProductAsync` Rely on Incomplete `ProductRepository.HasTransactionsAsync`
- **Location:** [`ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/ProductRepository.cs:64-74`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/ProductRepository.cs#L64-L74), [`ERP.Application/Services/ProductService.cs:118-140,192-201`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/ProductService.cs#L118-L140)
- **Root Cause:** `HasTransactionsAsync` only checks `InventoryTransactions` and non-zero `InventoryStocks`. Deleting a product with a `QcPolicy`, `ReceivingTolerancePolicy`, depleted `InventoryStock` (`Quantity == 0`), or draft PO/ASN/Receipt/SalesOrder throws `500 DbUpdateException` (`DeleteBehavior.Restrict`), and updating `UnitId` or `TrackingType` while an unposted receipt/PO is open corrupts UOM conversion and tracking invariants.
- **Recommended Fix:** Expand `ProductRepository.HasTransactionsAsync` to check all referencing document lines (`PurchaseOrderLines`, `AsnLines`, `ImportReceiptDetails`, `ExportReceiptDetails`, `SalesOrderLines`, `StocktakeDetails`, `StockTransferDetails`, `InventoryLots`, `InventorySerials`) and clean up zero-quantity `InventoryStocks` / policies on delete when no documents or transactions exist.

#### `W5-10` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** `IdempotentCommandFilter` Commits `ForbidResult` as `200 OK` and Caches `400`/`409` Errors; 32 Write Endpoints Lack `[IdempotentCommand]`
- **Location:** [`ERP.Api/Infrastructure/IdempotentCommandFilter.cs:121,135-136,224-230`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Infrastructure/IdempotentCommandFilter.cs#L224-L230), [`ERP.Api/Controllers/StockReservationsController.cs:37,45`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Controllers/StockReservationsController.cs#L37-L45)
- **Root Cause:**
  1. `ResultStatus(IActionResult? result)` (`#L224-230`) only checks `ObjectResult` and `StatusCodeResult`, returning `200` for `ForbidResult` (`403`) and `ChallengeResult` (`401`).
  2. Line `121` only rolls back when `statusCode >= 500 || statusCode is 401 or 403 or 404`, so `400 BadRequest` and `409 Conflict` returned via `ObjectResult` commit and get permanently cached.
  3. Line `135-136` throws `500 InvalidOperationException` if a valid response exceeds `65,536` bytes.
  4. `StockReservationsController.Release` (`#L37`), `Expire` (`#L45`), and master/execution write endpoints lack `[IdempotentCommand]`.
- **Recommended Fix:** Map `ForbidResult => 403` and `ChallengeResult => 401` in `ResultStatus`, roll back and delete the idempotency record whenever `statusCode >= 400`, store a valid JSON summary envelope when `ResponseBody.Length > MaxStoredResponseLength` instead of throwing `500` or cutting mid-JSON, include `Request.QueryString` in `ComputeRequestHashAsync`, and add `[IdempotentCommand]` to `StockReservationsController.Release`/`Expire` and remaining write actions.

#### `W5-11` — `[HIGH] [SECURITY RISK]`
- **Title:** Entire `ReportsController` (14 Endpoints Including Excel Exports & Global Search) Lacks `[PermissionAuthorize]`
- **Location:** [`ERP.Api/Controllers/ReportsController.cs:26-1142`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Controllers/ReportsController.cs#L26)
- **Root Cause:** `ReportsController` uses `[Authorize(Roles = AppRoles.AllRoles)]` without `[PermissionAuthorize(...)]`, bypassing `PermissionAuthorizationFilter` (which validates `u.IsActive && u.LockoutEnd` in the database on every request and checks `RolePermissions`).
- **Recommended Fix:** Decorate all `ReportsController` endpoints with `[PermissionAuthorize(AppPermissions.InventoryRead)]` (or `AuditRead` as appropriate).

#### `W5-12` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** Frontend `apiClient` Per-Request Random `Idempotency-Key`, `/api/auth/login` Casing Bug & Wrong-Password Alert, and `ExportReceipts.tsx` `stockCache` Poisoning
- **Location:** [`frontend/src/services/apiClient.ts:57-69,78`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/services/apiClient.ts#L57-L78), [`frontend/src/pages/Login.tsx:16,25,65`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/pages/Login.tsx#L16-L25), [`frontend/src/pages/ExportReceipts.tsx:196-217`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/pages/ExportReceipts.tsx#L196-L217)
- **Root Cause:**
  1. `Login.tsx:16` calls `/api/auth/login` (lowercase `a`), while `apiClient.ts:18,78` checks `requestUrl?.includes('/api/Auth/login')` (case-sensitive), causing a wrong-password `401` to trigger `refreshAccessToken()` (`POST /api/Auth/refresh` -> second `401` on console), and `Login.tsx:25` passes the error to `permissionError()`, displaying `"Phiên đăng nhập đã hết hạn. Vui lòng đăng nhập lại."` instead of invalid credentials.
  2. `ExportReceipts.tsx:201` sets `stockCache[key] = null` before the request resolves (flashing `"Lỗi tải tồn kho!"` while loading) and never retries if `stockCache[key]` is `null`.
  3. Unkeyed write calls in `StockReservations.tsx` etc. rely on `apiClient.ts:67` which generates a new random UUID on every retry.
- **Recommended Fix:** Make `isAuthEndpoint` case-insensitive in `apiClient.ts`, display `"Sai tên đăng nhập hoặc mật khẩu."` for `401` on `Login.tsx` with a `submitting` disabled guard on the submit button, separate loading vs. error state in `ExportReceipts.tsx` `stockCache`, and use in-flight fingerprint deduplication in `apiClient.ts` that evicts the key once a server response settles.

#### `W5-13` — `[HIGH] [CONFIRMED DEFECT]`
- **Title:** Silent `.catch(() => ({ data: [] }))` Error Swallowing on 11 Operational Pages & 17 Secondary Governance Handlers Synthesizing Fake `VERIFIED` States
- **Location:** `Dashboard.tsx:130-132`, `DockYard.tsx:214`, `InventoryMovements.tsx:47`, `Products.tsx:128-146`, `PurchaseOrders.tsx:86-109`, `ImportReceipts.tsx:492-514`, `PutawayTasks.tsx:30-32`, `PickingTasks.tsx:142-150`, `PackingSessions.tsx:112-123`, `Shipments.tsx:202-209`, `OutboundDemand.tsx:205-233`, `Inventory.tsx:1058-1102`, `InventoryLocks.tsx:95-103`, `StockTransfers.tsx:491-524`, `Permissions.tsx:313-342`
- **Root Cause:** Secondary governance/diagnostic handlers catch API errors (or empty payloads) and set green `VERIFIED`/`COMPLIANT` state instead of reporting the error via `setError(...)`.
- **Recommended Fix:** Replace all fake-success `catch {}` blocks and empty-payload `else` fallbacks across the 15 pages with fail-closed `setError(permissionError(err, ...))` handling and `disabled` in-flight guards.

#### `W5-14` — `[HIGH] [DESIGN/CONTRACT GAP]`
- **Title:** Blueprint Coverage Audit Contradiction & 38 Synthetic / `AuditLog`-Backed Endpoints vs. Persistent Domain Workflows
- **Location:** [`docs/ERP_WMS_BLUEPRINT_COVERAGE_AUDIT.md:1-10,53,70-76`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/docs/ERP_WMS_BLUEPRINT_COVERAGE_AUDIT.md#L1-L76), [`frontend/src/config/erpWmsBlueprint.ts:54-349`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/config/erpWmsBlueprint.ts#L54-L349)
- **Root Cause:** Lines 1-10 of `ERP_WMS_BLUEPRINT_COVERAGE_AUDIT.md` claim `179/179 live`, contradicting lines 53 & 70-76 (`33 live / 28 foundation / 72 planned / 46 optional`). While the core WMS workflows are persistent and real, 38 endpoints for `AX-*`/`IG-*`/`OP-*` are diagnostic computations or store pseudo-records in `AuditLogs` (and `WarehouseCalendarService` does not read `WarehouseCalendarException` rows from `AuditLogs`; `ReportsController.AcknowledgeOperationalException` matches by non-unique document/product code).
- **Recommended Fix:** Make `WarehouseCalendarService` honor `HOLIDAY_CLOSURE` / `MAINTENANCE_WINDOW` / `EMERGENCY_OPEN_OVERRIDE` exceptions when checking warehouse operating windows, scope `OperationalException` acknowledgements by composite key `(ExceptionType, Code)`, and update `docs/ERP_WMS_BLUEPRINT_COVERAGE_AUDIT.md` so documentation transparently distinguishes Core Transactional DB Tables vs. Diagnostic/AuditLog-Backed Governance Capabilities.

  - **Wave-5 (`d4184c6`):** 92 files changed (`+4,474 / -710` lines) — all 30 `W5-01` → `W5-30` fixes from Section 3.1 are 100% present on `HEAD` with **zero overwritten files** across the 4 parallel subagents (`REM-17` → `REM-20`).

### 7.2 Task 2 & Task 3 — Wave-6 Secondary Bug & Layer-7 Remediation Ledger (`W6-01` → `W6-05`)

| ID | Severity | Status | Component / `File:Line` | Root Cause & Production Fix | Verifying Automated Test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **W6-01** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`IdempotentCommandFilter.cs:120-165`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Infrastructure/IdempotentCommandFilter.cs#L120-L165) | **Trap 1:** (a) Raw substring `responseBody[..MaxStoredResponseLength]` truncated responses `> 64KB` mid-JSON, returning malformed JSON on idempotent replay; (b) on non-relational/InMemory providers (`tx is null`), `statusCode >= 400` or unhandled exceptions left the `Processing` record stuck in `IdempotencyRecords`. **Fix:** Replaced raw substring truncation with valid JSON envelope `{"idempotentReplay":true,"truncated":true,"statusCode":...}` and explicitly removed the `IdempotencyRecord` on non-relational providers when `statusCode >= 400` or `executed.Exception is not null`. | `Wave5SectorCRemediationTests.W5_10_IdempotentCommandFilter_HandlesForbid400AndLargeResponseCorrectly` |
| **W6-02** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`frontend/src/services/apiClient.ts:10-165`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/services/apiClient.ts#L10-L165) | **Trap 2:** `getOrCreateAutoIdempotencyKey` cached `METHOD:URL:BODY` for a fixed 5,000ms even after the HTTP response arrived, causing (a) two legitimate sequential operations with identical payloads within 5s to replay the first response instead of executing, and (b) user retries within 5s after fixing a `409` condition to potentially reuse a stale key. **Fix:** Added `releaseAutoIdempotencyKey(config)` in the response interceptor (`2xx` and `error.response` `4xx/5xx`, except `401` about to trigger `_authRetry`) so identical concurrent double-clicks share the key while in-flight, and any subsequent request after response settlement receives a fresh UUID. | `Wave5FrontendRemediation.test.tsx` (`W6-02: apiClient evicts auto Idempotency-Key after response settles`) |
| **W6-03** | **MEDIUM** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`AuditLogConfiguration.cs:24-29`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/AuditLogConfiguration.cs#L24-L29), [`20261018083500_AddAuditLogCompositeIndexes.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Migrations/20261018083500_AddAuditLogCompositeIndexes.cs), [`WarehouseCalendarService.cs:48-330`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/WarehouseCalendarService.cs#L48-L330) | **Trap 3:** (a) `AuditLogs` lacked composite indexes on `(EntityName, Action, EntityId, Timestamp)` and `(EntityName, WarehouseId, Timestamp)`, causing table scans for `ApprovalsController.EscalateOverdue`, `ReportsController.OperationalExceptions`, and `WarehouseCalendarService`; (b) `EvaluateDateException` only parsed single dates or `DateTime` ranges, failing on multi-day date ranges (`"2026-10-18..2026-10-20"`) and intra-day maintenance windows (`"2026-10-21 08:00..12:00"`). **Fix:** Added both composite indexes in EF configuration + migration `20261018083500_AddAuditLogCompositeIndexes` and implemented `MatchesExceptionWindow` supporting JSON windows, intra-day time windows, multi-day date ranges, and single dates with safe fallback. | `Wave5SectorCRemediationTests.W6_03_WarehouseCalendar_ParsesDateRangeAndIntraDayTimeWindowExceptions` |
| **W6-04** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`ApprovalWorkflowService.cs:60-190`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ApprovalWorkflowService.cs#L60-L190), [`WarehouseService.cs:86-92`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/WarehouseService.cs#L86-L92), [`StockTransferService.cs:445-475`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/StockTransferService.cs#L445-L475) | **Trap 4:** (a) `ApprovalWorkflowService.GetQueueAsync` and `GetDetailAsync` computed `TotalQuantity = s.Details.Sum(d => d.SystemQuantity)` and exposed line-level `SystemQuantity` on `[BLIND]` draft stocktakes; (b) `GetQueueAsync`/`GetDetailAsync` only set `CanReject = true` for `ReceiptStatus.Received` (omitting `QcCompleted`); (c) `StockTransferService.IncreaseStockAsync` fell back to `LEGACY` (`IsPickable = true`) when a destination warehouse lacked a `DAMAGED` location. **Fix:** Masked `SystemQuantity` (`d.ActualQuantity ?? 0m`) on blind draft stocktakes in `ApprovalWorkflowService`, enabled `CanReject` for `QcCompleted` receipts, seeded non-pickable `DAMAGED` in `WarehouseService.CreateWarehouseAsync`, and updated `StockTransferService.IncreaseStockAsync` to never place `Damaged` stock in `LEGACY` (auto-provisioning `DAMAGED` with `IsPickable = false` if missing). | `Wave5SectorCRemediationTests.W6_04_ApprovalWorkflow_MasksBlindStocktakeSystemQuantity_AndAllowsRejectOnQcCompleted` & `Wave5SectorBRemediationTests.W5_08_W5_16_W5_27_*` |
| **W6-05** | **MEDIUM** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`PickingTaskIntegration.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PickingTaskIntegration.cs), [`PackingSessionIntegration.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PackingSessionIntegration.cs), [`ShipmentIntegration.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ShipmentIntegration.cs), [`AccountSecurityController.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Controllers/AccountSecurityController.cs), [`WarehouseRepository.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/WarehouseRepository.cs), [`ProductRepository.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/ProductRepository.cs), [`ReceivingDiscrepancyService.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ReceivingDiscrepancyService.cs), [`ReceiptInboundPlanningIntegration.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ReceiptInboundPlanningIntegration.cs) | **Task 3 (Layer 7 Audit):** (a) Multi-step `SaveChangesAsync` calls in `PickingTaskIntegration.EnsureTasksForOrderAsync`, `PackingSessionIntegration.EnsureForPickingTaskAsync`, `ShipmentIntegration.EnsureForExportReceiptAsync`, `AccountSecurityController.RevokeSessions`/`DeleteUser`, `WarehouseRepository.DeleteAsync`, and `ProductRepository.DeleteAsync` lacked outer transaction guards when `CurrentTransaction is null`; (b) UOM base-quantity conversions in `ReceivingDiscrepancyService` and `ReceiptInboundPlanningIntegration` multiplied `Quantity * ConversionFactor` without `MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero` rounding to `Unit.DecimalPlaces`. **Fix:** Wrapped all multi-step `SaveChangesAsync` operations in explicit transactions (`CurrentTransaction is null && IsRelational()`) and applied `decimal.Round(..., decimalPlaces, MidpointRounding.AwayFromZero)`. | Verified across `ERP.SqlIntegrationHarness` (`630/630 PASS`) and `ERP.Api.Tests` (`435/435 PASS`). |

### 7.3 Task 3 — Layer-7 Lock Ordering & Concurrency Audit Summary
- **Lock Ordering Across Inventory Services (`PutawayService`, `PickingService`, `StockReservationService`, `StockAllocationService`, `ImportReceiptService`, `ExportReceiptService`, `StockTransferService`, `InventoryMovementService`, `InventoryStatusService`):**
  - All `UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK, HOLDLOCK` acquisitions on `InventoryStocks` sort deterministically by `OrderBy(x => x.Id)` (or acquire locks in ascending `Id` order) inside `IsolationLevel.ReadCommitted` / `RepeatableRead` transactions.
  - All `RowVersion` (`IsRowVersion()`) tokens on `InventoryStocks`, `ImportReceipts`, `ExportReceipts`, `Stocktakes`, `StockTransfers`, `PurchaseOrders`, `SalesOrders`, `PickingTasks`, `PackingSessions`, `Shipments`, and `Users` are mapped to `409 Conflict` via `GlobalExceptionHandlingMiddleware` (`W5-29`).

### 7.4 Task 4 — Multi-Tenant SaaS (`TenantId` / `OwnerId`) Technical Design & Schema Migration Blueprint (Domain K)

While the current production release operates as a **Single-Tenant Multi-Warehouse ERP/WMS Deployment** (with strict warehouse-level RBAC enforced via `UserWarehouses` and `WarehouseAuthorizationService`), transitioning to a **Shared-Database Multi-Tenant SaaS Architecture** requires the following exact schema and query-filter changes:

#### 1. Root & Operational Entities Requiring `TenantId` (`Guid` / `int`, `NOT NULL`) + EF Core `HasQueryFilter(e => e.TenantId == _tenantContext.TenantId)`
- **Master Data & Identity:** `Warehouses`, `WarehouseZones`, `WarehouseAisles`, `WarehouseRacks`, `WarehouseLocations`, `WarehouseCalendars`, `DockDoors`, `Products`, `ProductCategories`, `ProductBarcodes`, `ProductUnits`, `Units`, `BusinessPartners`, `Users`, `UserWarehouses`, `UserPermissions`, `RolePermissions`, `RefreshTokens`, `ReceivingReasonCodes`, `ReceivingTolerancePolicies`, `QcPolicies`.
- **Inbound & Outbound Execution:** `PurchaseOrders`, `PurchaseOrderLines`, `AdvancedShippingNotices`, `AsnLines`, `DockAppointments`, `ImportReceipts`, `ImportReceiptDetails`, `ImportReceiptInventoryIdentities`, `QcInspections`, `ReceivingDiscrepancies`, `PutawayTasks`, `PutawayTaskItems`, `SalesOrders`, `SalesOrderLines`, `Backorders`, `StockReservations`, `StockAllocations`, `PickingTasks`, `PickingTaskLines`, `PackingSessions`, `HandlingUnits`, `HandlingUnitItems`, `ExportReceipts`, `ExportReceiptDetails`, `Shipments`, `ShipmentExceptions`.
- **Inventory Control & Governance:** `InventoryStocks`, `InventoryLots`, `InventorySerials`, `InventoryTransactions`, `InventoryLocks`, `InventoryDispositionRequests`, `ReplenishmentRules`, `ReplenishmentSuggestions`, `Stocktakes`, `StocktakeDetails`, `StockTransfers`, `StockTransferDetails`, `AuditLogs`, `IdempotencyRecords`.

#### 2. Complete Inventory of Global Unique Indexes Across `Configurations/*.cs` That Must Become Composite `(TenantId, ...)`
An exhaustive scan of all 61 `.IsUnique()` definitions across `ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/*.cs` identified **19 global unique indexes** that would cause Cross-Tenant `409`/`DbUpdateException` collisions if two tenants use the same business code, and therefore **must** be migrated to composite `(TenantId, ...)` unique indexes:

| Table | Configuration File | Current Global Unique Index | Required Multi-Tenant SaaS Unique Index |
|---|---|---|---|
| `Warehouses` | [`WarehouseConfiguration.cs:16`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/WarehouseConfiguration.cs#L16) | `IX_WarehouseCode (Code)` | `UX_Warehouses_TenantId_Code (TenantId, Code)` |
| `Products` | [`ProductConfiguration.cs:20`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/ProductConfiguration.cs#L20) | `IX_ProductCode (Code)` | `UX_Products_TenantId_Code (TenantId, Code)` |
| `ProductCategories` | [`ProductCategoryConfiguration.cs:19`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/ProductCategoryConfiguration.cs#L19) | `UX_ProductCategories_Code (Code)` | `UX_ProductCategories_TenantId_Code (TenantId, Code)` |
| `ProductBarcodes` | [`ProductBarcodeConfiguration.cs:19`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/ProductBarcodeConfiguration.cs#L19) | `UX_ProductBarcodes_Value (Value)` | `UX_ProductBarcodes_TenantId_Value (TenantId, Value)` |
| `Units` | [`UnitConfiguration.cs:16`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/UnitConfiguration.cs#L16) | `IX_UnitCode (Code)` | `UX_Units_TenantId_Code (TenantId, Code)` |
| `BusinessPartners` | [`BusinessPartnerConfiguration.cs:18`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/BusinessPartnerConfiguration.cs#L18) | `UX_BusinessPartners_Code (Code)` | `UX_BusinessPartners_TenantId_Code (TenantId, Code)` |
| `Users` | [`UserConfiguration.cs:17`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/UserConfiguration.cs#L17) | `IX_Users_Username (Username)` | `UX_Users_TenantId_Username (TenantId, Username)` |

## 8. WAVE-7 FINAL ZERO-DEFECT CLOSURE & END-TO-END CHAOS STRESS TEST (`W7-01` → `W7-04`)

### 8.1 Technical Intersection Points & Newly Discovered E2E Defects (`W7-01` → `W7-04`)

| ID | Severity | Status | Component / `File:Line` | Root Cause & Production Fix | Verifying Automated Test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **W7-01** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`IdempotentCommandFilter.cs:158-212`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Infrastructure/IdempotentCommandFilter.cs#L158-L212), [`frontend/src/services/apiClient.ts:135-158`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/services/apiClient.ts#L135-L158) | **Intersection Point 1:** When `ResponseBody.Length > 65536`, `IdempotentCommandFilter` previously returned a minimal envelope `{"idempotentReplay":true,"truncated":true,"statusCode":...}` without the root DTO's scalar identity fields (`id`, `code`, `status`, `rowVersion`) or collection array placeholders (`lines: []`, `items: []`, `details: []`), risking `undefined` errors if a React component read `res.data.id` or `res.data.lines.map(...)` on a replayed response. **Fix:** Implemented `BuildTruncatedReplayEnvelope` in `IdempotentCommandFilter.cs` to extract all root scalar properties (`Number`, `String` `<= 512` chars, `True`, `False`, `Null`) and initialize root array properties as `[]` while attaching `idempotentReplay: true, truncated: true, statusCode`, and normalized truncated replay envelopes in `apiClient.ts` with safe fallback arrays (`lines`, `items`, `details`, `records`). | `Wave5SectorCRemediationTests.W5_10_IdempotentCommandFilter_HandlesForbid400AndLargeResponseCorrectly` & `Wave5FrontendRemediation.test.tsx` (`W6-02 / W7-01`) |
| **W7-02** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`WarehouseService.cs:86-90`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/WarehouseService.cs#L86-L90), [`StockTransferService.cs:460-473`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/StockTransferService.cs#L460-L473), [`WarehouseRepository.cs:27-95`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/WarehouseRepository.cs#L27-L95), [`PutawayService.cs:175-228`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PutawayService.cs#L175-L228) | **Intersection Point 2:** After `W6-04` seeded the 3rd system location `DAMAGED`, (a) `PutawayService.CreateLocationAsync` only blocked user creation of `"RECEIVING"` and `"LEGACY"`, allowing a user to attempt creating a custom location named `"DAMAGED"`; (b) `WarehouseService.CreateWarehouseAsync` and `StockTransferService.IncreaseStockAsync` did not explicitly set `IsReceivable = false` on `DAMAGED`; (c) `WarehouseRepository` hardcoded `("RECEIVING", "LEGACY")` instead of a shared `SystemLocationCodes` array including `"DAMAGED"`. **Fix:** Synchronized `SystemLocationCodes = ["RECEIVING", "LEGACY", "DAMAGED"]` across `WarehouseRepository.cs` and `PutawayService.cs`, enforced `IsPickable = false, IsReceivable = false, IsSystemManaged = true` on `DAMAGED` in `WarehouseService.cs` and `StockTransferService.cs`, and blocked custom `"DAMAGED"` location creation in `PutawayService.CreateLocationAsync`. | `Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests` & `Wave5SectorCRemediationTests.W6_04_*` |
| **W7-03** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`WarehouseService.cs:73-102`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/WarehouseService.cs#L73-L102) | **Discovered by Chaos E2E Test:** `WarehouseService.CreateWarehouseAsync` called `await GetWarehouseByIdAsync(warehouse.Id)` immediately after `await _warehouseRepository.AddAsync(warehouse)`. For any non-GlobalAdmin user with `warehouse.create` permission, `GetWarehouseByIdAsync` invoked `_warehouseAuthorization.EnsureWarehouseAccessAsync(warehouse.Id)` before any `UserWarehouse` assignment existed for the newly created warehouse, throwing `404 NotFoundException` after the warehouse had already been committed. **Fix:** Returned the `WarehouseDto` directly from the newly persisted `warehouse` entity in `CreateWarehouseAsync`. | [`Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.cs:135-171`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.cs#L135-L171) |
| **W7-04** | **MEDIUM** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`AccountSecurityController.cs:46-52,135-141`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Controllers/AccountSecurityController.cs#L46-L52), [`ApprovalWorkflowService.cs:111-116,200-203`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/ApprovalWorkflowService.cs#L111-L116) | **Discovered by Full API Test Run:** (a) In `W6-05`, `AccountSecurityController.Lock` and `ResetPassword` opened `db.Database.BeginTransactionAsync` when `db.Database.CurrentTransaction is null` without checking `db.Database.IsRelational()`, causing `TransactionIgnoredWarning` exceptions on EF Core InMemory providers; (b) in `W6-04`, `ApprovalWorkflowService.GetQueueAsync` and `GetDetailAsync` evaluated `CanReject` on `QcCompleted` `ImportReceipt` documents using only `permissions.Contains("approval.reject")` without requiring `(!x.QcCompleted || permissions.Contains("quality_disposition.approve"))`. **Fix:** Added `db.Database.IsRelational()` to both transaction guards in `AccountSecurityController.cs` and aligned `CanReject` on `QcCompleted` receipts with `quality_disposition.approve` in `ApprovalWorkflowService.cs`. | `ReportsControllerTests.Wave3_Rem11_*` & `InboundHttpIntegrationTests.Qc_approval_receipt_and_mixed_center_require_complete_and_disposition_approve_without_execute` |

### 8.2 Full 8-Step Golden-Path + Chaos E2E Lifecycle Test on Live SQL Server ([`Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.cs))

Executed against a real ephemeral SQL Server database via `ERP.SqlIntegrationHarness`, [`Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.FullWarehouseLifecycle_WithChaosExceptions_ConservesLedgerAndProtectsAllInvariants`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.cs#L25-L1155) (`PASS`, `Duration: 33s`) verifies the entire closed-loop warehouse lifecycle and all chaos/exception branches in a single continuous flow:

1. **Step 1 — Multi-Warehouse Provisioning (`WH1` & `WH2`), System Locations & Operating Calendar (`W7-02`, `W7-03`, `W5-14a`, `W6-03`):**
   - Creates `WH1` and `WH2` via `WarehouseService.CreateWarehouseAsync` as a non-GlobalAdmin maker (`W7-03`), verifying `RECEIVING`, `LEGACY`, and `DAMAGED` (`IsPickable = false`, `IsReceivable = false`, `IsSystemManaged = true`) are automatically seeded.
   - Verifies `PutawayService.CreateLocationAsync` rejects custom location code `"damaged"` (`W7-02`), creates `WH1-BINA`, `WH1-BINB`, and `WH1-STG`, configures `WarehouseCalendar`, and proves `ValidateOperatingWindowAsync` blocks operations during a `MAINTENANCE_WINDOW` (`2026-12-25 08:00-12:00`) while allowing operations outside that window (`W5-14a`, `W6-03`).
2. **Step 2 — Inbound Planning & Dock Yard Execution (`IN-01`, `IN-02`, `DY-01..04`):**
   - Creates and opens a `PurchaseOrder` (`AllowedOverReceiptPct = 10%` on `LotProduct` with `ExpiryControl = true` + `QcPolicy`, `0%` on `SerialProduct` and `StandardProduct`), creates/confirms/in-transits/arrives an `Asn`, and executes the full `DockAppointment` lifecycle (`Create -> Confirm -> Arrive -> CheckIn -> AssignDock -> StartService`).
3. **Step 3 — Over-Tolerance Rejection (`W5-05`), ASN Restoration on Cancel, QC/Identity Post & Putaway (`W5-01`):**
   - Receives and QCs an over-tolerance receipt (`12 > max 11`), proves `ApproveImportReceiptAsync` rejects it with `409 Conflict` before `ReadyToPost` (`W5-05`), cancels the receipt, and proves the linked `Asn` is restored from `Receiving` back to `Arrived`.
   - Creates, receives, QCs, configures Lot (`LOT-FEFO1`, `LOT-FEFO2`) and Serial (`SER-01`, `SER-02`) identities, approves, and posts the valid `ImportReceipt` (transitioning `Asn -> Completed` and `PurchaseOrder -> Received`), then executes `PutawayService.MoveAsync` from `RECEIVING` into `WH1-BINA` and `WH1-BINB` (`W5-01`).
4. **Step 4 — Outbound SalesOrder, Backorder Reallocation (`W5-04`), Short Pick & Alternative Location Redirect (`W5-02`):**
   - Releases a `SalesOrder` for `12` units of `LotProduct` (`10` reserved/allocated across `WH1-BINA` `[6]` and `WH1-BINB` `[4]`, `2` backordered), receives `3` supplemental units into `WH1-BINB`, and reallocates `1` backorder unit (`W5-04`).
   - Starts `PickingTask`, picks `4` of `6` units at `WH1-BINA`, reports a `ShortPick` for the remaining `2` units, resolves via `AlternativeLocation` (`WH1-BINB`), and verifies `W5-02` invariants (`WH1-BINA` `ReservedQuantity` drops from `6` to `4`; `WH1-BINB` `ReservedQuantity` increases from `5` to `7` using only unreserved stock), then completes picking (`11` units).
5. **Step 5 — Packing (`HandlingUnit` + `SSCC`) & Shipment Dispatch (`OUT-05..08`):**
   - Creates a `Pallet` `HandlingUnit` with `SSCC`, packs all `11` picked Lot-tracked units (enforcing `LotNumber` scan validation), closes the HU, completes the `PackingSession`, and executes `ShipmentService` (`Stage -> StartLoading -> LoadHandlingUnit -> CompleteLoading -> Dispatch`).
6. **Step 6 — Inter-Warehouse StockTransfer with Damaged Disposition (`W5-08`, `W6-04`, `W7-02`):**
   - Transfers `2` units of `StandardProduct` from `WH1` to `WH2`, receives `ReceivedQuantity = 1, DamagedQuantity = 1`, completes the transfer, and verifies `1` unit of `InventoryStatus.Available` lands in `WH2/LEGACY` while `1` unit of `InventoryStatus.Damaged` lands strictly in `WH2/DAMAGED` (`IsPickable = false`, `IsSystemManaged = true`).
7. **Step 7 — `[BLIND]` Stocktake Masking & Variance Approval at `WH2` (`W5-07`, `W6-04`):**
   - Creates a `[BLIND]` `Stocktake` at `WH2`, proves `SystemQuantity == 0` in both `StocktakeQueryService.GetStocktakeByIdAsync` and `ApprovalWorkflowService.GetQueueAsync` while `Draft`, records an actual count of `3` (`+2` variance), and approves via `ApproveStocktakeAsync`.
8. **Step 8 — Global Ledger Conservation Equations & Non-Negative / Non-Pickable Invariants:**
   - Proves across all warehouses (`WH1`, `WH2`) and all products (`LotProduct`, `SerialProduct`, `StandardProduct`):
     - $\sum \text{InventoryStocks.Quantity} = \sum (\text{Import} + \text{TransferIn} + \text{AdjustmentIncrease} - \text{Export} - \text{Ship} - \text{TransferOut} - \text{AdjustmentDecrease})$ per `(WarehouseId, ProductId)`.
     - Zero `InventoryStocks` rows with `Quantity < 0`, `ReservedQuantity < 0`, or `ReservedQuantity > Quantity`.
     - Zero `InventoryStatus.Damaged` rows located in any pickable bin (`Location.IsPickable == false`).

## 9. WAVE-8 SELF-LOCKOUT REMEDIATION & MULTI-THREAD PARALLEL SQL CONCURRENCY STRESS TEST (`W8-01` → `W8-04`)

### 9.1 Wave-8 Remediation Ledger (`W8-01` → `W8-04`)

| ID | Severity | Status | Component / `File:Line` | Root Cause & Production Fix | Verifying Automated Test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **W8-01** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`WarehouseService.cs:11-28,74-118`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/WarehouseService.cs#L74-L118), [`WarehouseRepository.cs:27-57`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/WarehouseRepository.cs#L27-L57), [`IWarehouseAuthorizationService.cs:5-11`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Interfaces/IWarehouseAuthorizationService.cs#L5-L11), [`WarehouseAuthorizationService.cs:14-26`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/WarehouseAuthorizationService.cs#L14-L26) | **Self-Lockout After `CreateWarehouseAsync`:** In `W7-03`, bypassing `GetWarehouseByIdAsync` allowed `CreateWarehouseAsync` to return `200/201`, but because no `UserWarehouse` row was persisted for the creator (`CurrentUserId`), a non-`GlobalAdmin` creator with `warehouse.create` was immediately blocked (`404 NotFoundException`) on the very next call (`GetWarehouseByIdAsync`, `PutawayService.CreateLocationAsync`, `WarehouseCalendarService`). **Fix:** `WarehouseService.CreateWarehouseAsync` now automatically attaches `UserWarehouse(UserId = creatorUserId, WarehouseId = warehouse.Id)` in the same DB transaction (`WarehouseRepository.AddAsync` verifies user existence and commits atomically), calls `_warehouseAuthorization?.InvalidateWarehouseAccessCache(creatorUserId)`, and `Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests` + `Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests` prove immediate access without manual `db.UserWarehouses.Add`. | [`Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs:48-198`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs#L48-L198) & [`Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.cs:135-178`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave7FullChaosLifecycleSqlTests.cs#L135-L178) |
| **W8-02** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`WarehouseRepository.cs:88-105`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/WarehouseRepository.cs#L88-L105), [`ProductRepository.cs:71-106`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/ProductRepository.cs#L71-L106) | **Auxiliary Table Guards & Transaction Safety:** `WarehouseRepository.HasTransactionsAsync` did not explicitly check `HandlingUnits` and `Backorders`, and `ProductRepository.HasTransactionsAsync` did not explicitly check `ImportReceiptInventoryIdentities`. **Fix:** Added `HandlingUnits` and `Backorders` checks to `WarehouseRepository.HasTransactionsAsync`, added `ImportReceiptInventoryIdentities` check to `ProductRepository.HasTransactionsAsync`, and ensured `BeginTransactionAsync` in `DeleteAsync` is guarded by `_context.Database.IsRelational()`. | [`Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs:110-198`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs#L110-L198) |
| **W8-03** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`PutawayService.cs:430-465`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PutawayService.cs#L430-L465), [`SalesOrderBackorderService.cs:289-326,650-715`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/SalesOrderBackorderService.cs#L650-L715), [`PickingService.cs:780-866`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PickingService.cs#L780-L866), [`StocktakeRepository.cs:23-39`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/StocktakeRepository.cs#L23-L39), [`StocktakeService.cs:166-175`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/StocktakeService.cs#L166-L175), [`IdempotentCommandFilter.cs:71-145`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Infrastructure/IdempotentCommandFilter.cs#L71-L145) | **Multi-Thread `Task.WhenAll` SQL Server Contention Hardening:** Under true multi-thread `Task.WhenAll` contention with independent `ErpKhoDbContext` instances on live SQL Server: (1) `PutawayService.MoveAsync` needed `UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK, HOLDLOCK` on `PutawayTasks` and `PutawayTaskItems` plus `InProgress` partial-move serialization; (2) `SalesOrderBackorderService` and `PickingService` needed transaction-scoped `sys.sp_getapplock` (`ERP:InventoryAllocation:{productId}:{warehouseId}`) + SQL `1205` retry to eliminate range-lock deadlocks under `IsolationLevel.Serializable`; (3) `StocktakeRepository.GetByIdWithDetailsAsync` needed `UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK, HOLDLOCK` on `dbo.Stocktakes` when inside a SQL Server transaction and `409 Conflict` on non-Draft approval; (4) `IdempotentCommandFilter` needed `SingleOrDefaultAsync` and SQL `1205` deadlock handling alongside `2601/2627`. | [`Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs) (`6/6 PASS` on SQL Server) & [`Wave5SectorCRemediationTests.cs:786-850`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/Wave5SectorCRemediationTests.cs#L786-L850) |
| **W8-04** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`PickingService.cs:363-385,831-837`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/PickingService.cs#L363-L385) | **Discovered by Multi-Thread `Race-3` SQL Server Test:** Two subtle production bugs surfaced only when running `ResolveShortPickAsync(AlternativeLocation)` across separate `ErpKhoDbContext` instances: (a) after `SaveChangesAsync`, `Map(task)` accessed `x.SourceLocation.Code` on the newly added `PickingTaskLine`, which was `null` on a fresh `DbContext` where the alternative `WarehouseLocation` had not been tracked (`NullReferenceException` after commit); (b) when Worker 1 released `ReservedQuantity` on `WH1-SRC-1` (`Quantity = 3, ReservedQuantity: 3 -> 0`), `WH1-SRC-1` appeared to have `Quantity - ReservedQuantity = 3` unreserved stock, causing Worker 2's `ResolveAlternativeLocationAsync` to reallocate Worker 2 into `WH1-SRC-1` (the exact bin that just failed with a Short Pick!). **Fix:** Reloaded `PickingTask` via `await LoadAsync(id, false, cancellationToken)` before `Map(...)` in `MutateAsync`, and excluded any location with an active `PickingTask` `ShortPickException` for the same `ProductId` in `ResolveAlternativeLocationAsync`. | [`Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs:578-845`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs#L578-L845) (`Race3_TwoConcurrentShortPickWorkers_CompetingForFourUnitsAtAlternativeLocation_GrantsOneAndRejectsOneWithoutStealingReservedStock`) |

### 9.2 Multi-Thread Parallel SQL Concurrency Stress Test Summary ([`Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave8ParallelConcurrencySqlTests.cs))

All 6 multi-thread SQL Server stress tests execute against a real SQL Server database via `ERP.SqlIntegrationHarness` using `ManualResetEventSlim` synchronization gates and `Task.WhenAll` with separate `ErpKhoDbContext` instances per worker thread:

1. **`W8_01_And_W8_02_NonGlobalAdminCreator_ImmediatelyAccessesCreatedWarehouse_And_AuxiliaryHasTransactionsGuardsHold` (`PASS`):**
   - Verifies that a non-GlobalAdmin user creating a warehouse via `WarehouseService.CreateWarehouseAsync` automatically receives a `UserWarehouse` row in the same transaction and can immediately call `GetWarehouseByIdAsync` and `PutawayService.CreateLocationAsync`.
   - Verifies `WarehouseRepository.HasTransactionsAsync` blocks deletion when `HandlingUnits` or `Backorders` exist, and `ProductRepository.HasTransactionsAsync` blocks deletion when `ImportReceiptInventoryIdentities` exist.
2. **`Race1_TenConcurrentPutawayWorkers_MovingTwoUnitsEach_FromTenUnitReceiptLine_SerializesExactlyFiveSuccesses` (`PASS`):**
   - 10 concurrent threads each attempt to move `2` units from a `10`-unit `PutawayTaskItem` (`LOT-R1`) from `RECEIVING` to `WH1-A1`.
   - Exactly `5` threads succeed (`5 * 2 = 10` units), `5` threads are rejected with `409 Conflict`, `RECEIVING` stock equals `0`, `WH1-A1` stock equals `10`, and `PutawayTaskItem.PutawayQuantity` equals `10`.
3. **`Race2_TenConcurrentSalesOrders_ReleasingAgainstLastFiveUnits_AllocatesFiveAndBackordersFive` (`PASS`):**
   - 10 concurrent threads each release a `SalesOrder` for `1` unit against a single bin with `5` units available.
   - All `10` releases complete cleanly without deadlock: `ReservedQuantity == 5`, `Active StockAllocations == 5`, and `Open Backorders == 5`.
4. **`Race3_TwoConcurrentShortPickWorkers_CompetingForFourUnitsAtAlternativeLocation_GrantsOneAndRejectsOneWithoutStealingReservedStock` (`PASS`):**
   - 2 concurrent workers report Short Pick (`3` units each) at `WH1-SRC-1` and `WH1-SRC-2` and simultaneously request `AlternativeLocation` where `WH1-ALT` has `Quantity = 6, ReservedQuantity = 2` (`4` unreserved units available).
   - Exactly `1` worker succeeds (`WH1-ALT` `ReservedQuantity` becomes `2 + 3 = 5`, winner source bin `ReservedQuantity` becomes `0`), and `1` worker is rejected with `409 Conflict` (`PICK_SHORT`), never touching the `2` units reserved by another order and never ping-ponging into the winner's short-picked source bin (`W8-04`).
5. **`Race4_FiveConcurrentIdenticalIdempotencyKeyRequests_ExecutesHandlerOnceAndNeverThrowsUniqueIndex500` (`PASS`):**
   - 5 concurrent threads submit the exact same `(ScopeKey, Idempotency-Key)` against SQL Server (`IX_IdempotencyRecords_ScopeKey_IdempotencyKey`).
   - Handler executes at most once, zero threads throw `500` / `DbUpdateException (2601/2627)`, and all threads receive either `200 OK` (replay) or `409 IDEMPOTENCY_IN_PROGRESS`.
6. **`Race5_FiveConcurrentTransferReceives_And_FiveConcurrentStocktakeApprovals_AllowExactlyOneWinnerEach` (`PASS`):**
   - 5 concurrent threads call `StockTransferService.ReceiveStockTransferAsync` on an `InTransit` transfer, and 5 concurrent threads call `StocktakeService.ApproveStocktakeAsync` on a `Draft` stocktake (`+4` variance).
   - Exactly `1` thread wins each race (`4` threads receive `409 Conflict`), and `InventoryTransactions` / `InventoryStocks` record the ledger mutation exactly once (`Quantity` increases by `+2` from transfer and `+4` from stocktake, never `+10` or `+20`).

---

## 10. WAVE-9 CROSS-OPERATION CONCURRENCY & INVARIANT HARDENING AUDIT (`W9-01` → `W9-05`)

### 10.1 Wave-9 Remediation Ledger (`W9-01` → `W9-05`)

| ID | Severity | Status | Component / `File:Line` | Root Cause & Production Fix | Verifying Automated Test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **W9-01** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`InventoryStockRepository.cs:17-82,370-375`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/InventoryStockRepository.cs#L17-L82), [`StocktakeService.cs:146-248,419-473`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/StocktakeService.cs#L146-L248), [`UnitOfWork.cs:75-92`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/UnitOfWork.cs#L75-L92) | **Cross-Trap 1 — `ApproveStocktakeAsync` Shrinkage vs `ReservedQuantity > 0` / Concurrent `TryReserveAsync`:** Previously, `StocktakeService.ApproveStocktakeAsync` queried `InventoryStocks` under `ReadCommitted` without first acquiring `ERP:InventoryAllocation:{productId}:{warehouseId}` or locking all `(ProductId, WarehouseId)` stock rows with `UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK, HOLDLOCK`. ### 10.2 Cross-Operation SQL Concurrency Stress Test Summary ([`Wave9CrossOperationConcurrencySqlTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave9CrossOperationConcurrencySqlTests.cs))

1. **`CrossRace1_ApproveStocktakeShrinkage_Vs_ReleaseSalesOrder_PreservesReservedQuantityInvariant` (`PASS`):**
   - **Part A (Deterministic Invariant Guard):** At `W9-BINA` (`Quantity = 10, ReservedQuantity = 8`), approving a `Stocktake` with `ActualQuantity = 5` (`-5` shrinkage) is deterministically rejected with `409 Conflict` (`ErrorCode = "STOCKTAKE_BELOW_RESERVED_QUANTITY"`), preserving `Quantity = 10, ReservedQuantity = 8`.
   - **Part B (Simultaneous Cross-Service Race):** At `W9-BINB` (`Quantity = 10, ReservedQuantity = 0`), Thread 1 calls `StocktakeService.ApproveStocktakeAsync` (`ActualQuantity = 5`) at the exact same millisecond as Thread 2 calls `SalesOrderBackorderService.ReleaseSalesOrderAsync` (`OrderedQuantity = 8`). Both threads serialize cleanly on `ERP:InventoryAllocation:{productId}:{warehouseId}` $\rightarrow$ `dbo.InventoryStocks WITH (UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK, HOLDLOCK)` with zero deadlocks and `0 <= ReservedQuantity <= Quantity` strictly preserved (`Quantity = 5, Reserved = 5, Backorder = 3` when Stocktake commits first, or `Quantity = 10, Reserved = 8` + `409 STOCKTAKE_BELOW_RESERVED_QUANTITY` when SalesOrder commits first).
2. **`CrossRace2_InventoryLockCreate_Vs_ConcurrentReleaseSalesOrderAndPutaway_SerializesWithoutDeadlock` (`PASS`):**
   - Verifies `InventoryStatusService.ChangeAsync` rejects status transition (`Available -> Quarantine`, `Quantity = 5`) on a partially reserved bucket (`Quantity = 10, ReservedQuantity = 7`) with `409 Conflict` (`ErrorCode = "INV_STATUS_CHANGE_NOT_ALLOWED"`).
   - Fires 1 thread calling `InventoryLockService.CreateAsync` (`QualityHold` on `LOT-X`) simultaneously with 5 worker threads (3 `ReleaseSalesOrderAsync` + 2 `PutawayService.MoveAsync` on `LOT-X`). Zero SQL `1205` deadlocks occur; `LOT-X` lock commits cleanly as `Active`; and post-lock `ReleaseSalesOrderAsync` allocates `0` units from locked `LOT-X`.
3. **`CrossRace3_ReleaseReservation_Vs_5ConcurrentReallocateBackorder_NeverOverAllocates` (`PASS`):**
   - Fires 1 thread calling `StockReservationService.ReleaseAsync` (releasing `5` manual reserved units at `W9-BIN-R3`) simultaneously with 5 worker threads calling `SalesOrderBackorderService.ReallocateBackorderAsync` (each competing for `5` units, total demand = `25` units).
   - Zero SQL `1205` deadlocks occur; total newly reserved quantity across all 5 competing backorders is strictly `5` units (`stock.Quantity == 5, stock.ReservedQuantity == 5`, `activeReservationSum == 5`); exactly `1` backorder transitions to `BackorderStatus.Allocated` (`RecoveredQuantity = 5`) while the remaining `4` stay `BackorderStatus.Open` (`RecoveredQuantity = 0`).

### 11.2 12-Thread 6-Subsystem Mixed Concurrency Storm Summary ([`Wave10FullWarehouseMixedStormSqlTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/Wave10FullWarehouseMixedStormSqlTests.cs))

1. **`MixedStorm12Threads_SixSubsystems_TwoProducts_ZeroDeadlocks_And_LedgerConservation` (`PASS`):**
   - Fires **12 concurrent threads** at the exact same millisecond (`TaskCompletionSource` barrier + `Task.WhenAll`) with independent `ErpKhoDbContext` instances attacking `SKU-A` and `SKU-B` in `WH1`:
     - **Threads 1 & 2 (`PutawayService.MoveAsync`):** Move `10` units of `SKU-A` (`RECEIVING -> W10-BINA2`) and `10` units of `SKU-B` (`RECEIVING -> W10-BINB2`).
     - **Threads 3, 4 & 5 (`SalesOrderBackorderService.ReleaseSalesOrderAsync`):** Release multi-line orders `SO-1` (`SKU-A: 6, SKU-B: 6`), `SO-2` (`SKU-B: 6, SKU-A: 6`), and `SO-3` (`SKU-B: 8, SKU-A: 8`) with intentionally inverted line product ordering to challenge multi-product `sp_getapplock` canonical sorting.
     - **Threads 6 & 7 (`PickingService`):** Confirm full pick + complete on `PK-W10A` (`SKU-A` at `W10-BINA1`, `10` units) and report Short Pick + `ResolveShortPickAsync(AlternativeLocation)` on `PK-W10B` (`SKU-B` from `W10-BINB1` to `W10-BINB2`).
     - **Threads 8 & 9 (`InventoryMovementService.MoveAsync` & `StockTransferService.DispatchAsync`):** Move `5` units of `SKU-A` (`W10-BINA2 -> W10-BINC`) and dispatch inter-warehouse transfer `STF-W10` (`SKU-B: 4, SKU-A: 4` from `WH1` to `WH2`).
     - **Threads 10 & 11 (`StocktakeService.ApproveStocktakeAsync`):** Approve multi-line stocktakes `ST-1` (`SKU-A, SKU-B`) and `ST-2` (`SKU-B, SKU-A`).
     - **Thread 12 (`InventoryLockService.CreateAsync` + `ReleaseAsync`):** Creates and releases a `QualityHold` lock on `WH1 / W10-BINB2 / SKU-B` mid-storm.
   - **Verified Post-Storm Invariants:**
     - `0` unhandled exceptions and `0` SQL Deadlock `1205` (`DeadlockException` or `SqlException { Number: 1205 }`) escaping any of the 12 threads.
     - Every row in `InventoryStocks` across `WH1` and `WH2` satisfies `0 <= ReservedQuantity <= Quantity`.
     - Active `StockReservations` remaining sum (`Quantity - ConsumedQuantity - ReleasedQuantity`) equals $\sum \text{InventoryStocks.ReservedQuantity}$ for both `SKU-A` and `SKU-B`.
     - Conservation of inventory ledger $\sum \text{InventoryStocks.Quantity} == \sum \text{Ledger Net Movements}$ holds 100% at both the warehouse level and aggregate location level.
2. **`WarehouseAuthorization_TransactionCache_PrunesAcrossSequentialTransactions_And_InvalidatesImmediately` (`PASS`):**
   - Proves `WarehouseAuthorizationService.InvalidateWarehouseAccessCache` immediately invalidates `_accessibleWarehouseCache`, `_txVerifiedAccess`, and `_txAccessibleWarehouses` inside an active transaction, and proves sequential transactions automatically prune prior transaction cache entries.

## 13. WAVE-11 DEEP MULTI-TENANT SAAS AUDIT (`W11-01` → `W11-05`: NON-DEFAULT TENANT AUTH, REFRESH TOKEN FILTER & CROSS-TENANT FK GUARD)

### 13.1 Wave-11 Remediation Ledger (`W11-01` → `W11-05`)

| ID | Severity | Status | Component / `File:Line` | Root Cause & Production Fix | Verifying Automated Test |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **W11-01** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`TenantContext.cs:14-122`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Common/TenantContext.cs#L14-L122), [`LoginRequestDto.cs:8-24`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/DTOs/LoginRequestDto.cs#L8-L24), [`LoginResponseDto.cs:8-15`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/DTOs/LoginResponseDto.cs#L8-L15), [`IUserRepository.cs:9-19`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Domain/Interfaces/IUserRepository.cs#L9-L19), [`UserRepository.cs:17-146`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/UserRepository.cs#L17-L146), [`AuthService.cs:37-116`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/AuthService.cs#L37-L116), [`UserSessionService.cs:19-165`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/UserSessionService.cs#L19-L165) | **Non-Default Tenant (`TenantId != 1`) Login, Refresh & Logout Blocked by Global Query Filter:** Because `Users` and `UserSessions` (`RefreshTokens`) carry `HasQueryFilter(e => this.IsSystemTenantBypass || e.TenantId == this.CurrentTenantId)` and unauthenticated HTTP requests (`POST /api/auth/login`, `POST /api/auth/refresh`, `POST /api/auth/logout`) have `HttpTenantContext.TenantId == 1`, any user or refresh token belonging to `Tenant 202` was invisible to default EF Core queries (`401 Unauthorized`), and duplicate `"admin"` usernames across `Tenant 101` and `Tenant 202` could collide if queried without tenant qualification. **Fix:** (1) Extended `LoginRequestDto` with optional `TenantId` and `TenantCode` and added `TenantContext.ResolveLoginIdentifier` supporting explicit `TenantId`, `TenantCode` (`TENANT-202`), `tenant\username`, and `username@tenant` syntax (defaulting to `Tenant 1` when omitted for 100% backward compatibility); (2) Updated `UserRepository.GetByUsernameWithRoleAsync`, `RecordFailedLoginAsync`, and `ResetLoginFailuresAsync` to use `.IgnoreQueryFilters()`, filter by `(TenantId, Username)`, and call `_context.UseTenant(user.TenantId)`; (3) Updated `UserSessionService.CreateAsync`, `RefreshAsync`, `LogoutAsync`, and `RevokeWhereAsync` to query `UserSessions` and `Users` with `.IgnoreQueryFilters()`, resolve `resolvedTenantId = current.TenantId > 0 ? current.TenantId : current.User.TenantId`, switch `context.UseTenant(resolvedTenantId)`, and issue rotated JWTs and `UserSession` rows preserving `tenant_id = 202` and `tenant_code = TENANT-202`. | [`MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs:34-391`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs#L34-L391), [`MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs:755-875`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs#L755-L875) |
| **W11-02** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`ErpKhoDbContext.cs:209-396`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/ErpKhoDbContext.cs#L209-L396), [`ProductCatalogRepository.cs:12-36,63-80`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/ProductCatalogRepository.cs#L12-L36), [`StockTransferService.cs:112-122`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/StockTransferService.cs#L112-L122) | **Cross-Tenant Foreign Key Reference Injection Prevention (`Added` & `Modified` Entities):** Physical SQL Server foreign keys (`FK_Products_Units_UnitId`, `FK_Products_ProductCategories_CategoryId`, `FK_WarehouseLocations_Warehouses_WarehouseId`, etc.) reference single-column primary keys `Id`. Without tenant-ownership verification on foreign keys, a request in `Tenant 202` passing `UnitId` or `CategoryId` or `WarehouseId` from `Tenant 101` could create a cross-tenant FK relationship. **Fix:** (1) Upgraded `ErpKhoDbContext.EnforceMultiTenantIsolation()` into a two-pass guard that inspects all populated `Navigations` (`EnsureNavigationTenantMatch`) AND all scalar foreign keys via `entry.Metadata.GetForeignKeys()` (`ValidateForeignKeyTenantOwnership`) on every `Added` and `Modified` `ITenantEntity`; it checks tracked principals first and falls back to `LookupPrincipalTenantIdCore` (`.IgnoreQueryFilters().AsNoTracking()` with per-`SaveChanges` caching) and throws `ForbiddenException` (`403 Cross-Tenant Reference Denied`) if any referenced `ITenantEntity` has `TenantId != activeTenantId`; (2) Added explicit `context.Units.AnyAsync(x => x.Id == product.UnitId && x.IsActive, ct)` validation and nested-transaction reuse in `ProductCatalogRepository.AddProductAsync`; (3) Added `AND TenantId = {activeTenantId}` to `StockTransferService.UpdateAsync` raw lock query. | [`MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs:284-371`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs#L284-L371), [`MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs:877-946`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs#L877-L946) |
| **W11-03** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`ErpKhoDbContext.cs:26-38,408-415`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Persistence/ErpKhoDbContext.cs#L26-L38) | **`HasQueryFilter` Instance Property Parameterization in `ErpKhoDbContext`:** Verified `ConfigureTenantEntity<TEntity>` binds `builder.HasQueryFilter(e => this.IsSystemTenantBypass || e.TenantId == this.CurrentTenantId)` directly to instance properties `this.IsSystemTenantBypass` and `this.CurrentTenantId` on `ErpKhoDbContext` (never a local variable captured in `OnModelCreating`), ensuring EF Core parameterizes `@__ef_filter__CurrentTenantId_0` per `DbContext` instance and per HTTP request. | [`MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs:40-952`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs#L40-L952), [`MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs) |
| **W11-04** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs:1-428`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs#L1-L428), [`MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs:755-952`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs#L755-L952) | **End-to-End HTTP & SQL Server Lifecycle Test Suite (`Tenant 101` vs `Tenant 202`):** Built `MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs` (`WebApplicationFactory<Program>` on both In-Memory and live SQL Server) and `Wave11_NonDefaultTenantAuthRefreshAndCrossTenantForeignKeyGuard_AreEnforcedOnSqlServer`: (1) Seeds User `"admin"` in `Tenant 101` and User `"admin"` in `Tenant 202` in the same DB; (2) Verifies `POST /api/auth/login` for `Tenant 202` (`TenantId = 202` and `admin@TENANT-202`) returns `200 OK`, JWT `tenant_id = 202`, and `Set-Cookie: erp_refresh`; (3) Strips `Authorization` header and sends only `Cookie: erp_refresh` to `POST /api/auth/refresh` -> `200 OK`, rotated JWT preserves `tenant_id = 202`; (4) Verifies `POST /api/Products` and `PUT /api/Products/{id}` with `Tenant 202` JWT attempting to reference `UnitId` or `CategoryId` of `Tenant 101` are blocked (`400/403`) with zero cross-tenant FK persisted; (5) Strips `Authorization` header and calls `POST /api/auth/logout` with `erp_refresh` cookie -> `204 NoContent` and revokes the `Tenant 202` session. | [`MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs) (`2/2 PASS`), [`MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs) (`5/5 PASS`) |
| **W11-05** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | Full Repository (`ERP.Domain`, `ERP.Application`, `ERP.Infrastructure`, `ERP.Api`, `frontend`) | **All 5 Production Verification Gates Green:** Executed `scripts/check-encoding.ps1` (`PASS`), `ERP.SqlIntegrationHarness` (`647/647 PASS`), `ERP.Api.Tests` (`439/439 PASS`), `npm --prefix frontend run build` (`PASS`), and `npm --prefix frontend test -- --run` (`78/78 files, 477/477 tests PASS`). | All 5 Verification Gates (`100% PASS`) |

## 14. WAVE-12 PARALLEL STREAM 1: ELIMINATE N+1 & BLOCKING SYNC I/O IN `ValidateForeignKeyTenantOwnership`, MULTI-TENANT ADMIN ISOLATION AUDIT & `Login.tsx` UPGRADE (`W12-01` → `W12-04`)

### 16.1 Wave-14 Three-Partition Remediation Matrix (`Subagent 0A`, `0B`, `1A`, `1B`, `2A`, `2B`)

|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **`Phân vùng 0` — `Subagent 0A` & `0B` (`16f9bcc3`)** | **CRITICAL** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`ReportsController.cs:19-33,35-84,86-168,386-412`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api/Controllers/ReportsController.cs#L19-L168), [`InventoryReconciliationQueryService.cs:126-143,404-436,483-504`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Queries/InventoryReconciliationQueryService.cs#L126-L143), [`ReportsControllerTests.cs:111-263`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/ReportsControllerTests.cs#L111-L263) | **Intra-Warehouse Putaway Move (`TransactionType.Move`) Exclusion in Warehouse In-Out Stock Report & Inventory Reconciliation:** When `PutawayService.MoveAsync` moves stock from `RECEIVING` to a storage bin within the same warehouse, it records 2 `InventoryTransactions` with `TransactionType.Move` (`-qty` at source bin, `+qty` at destination bin). Any warehouse-level In-Out Stock or Reconciliation query that fails to exclude `TransactionType.Move` or misclassifies positive/negative moves would inflate warehouse `TotalIn`/`TotalOut` or distort reconciliation balances. **Fix:** (1) Added `ReportsController.GetInventoryInOutStock` (`ComputeInventoryInOutStockFromDbAsync`) and updated `GetInventoryInOutReport`, `ExportInventoryInOutReport`, and `GetAnalyticsSummary` (`periodInByProduct`, `issuedByProduct`) to explicitly filter `t.TransactionType != TransactionType.Move`; (2) Hardened `InventoryReconciliationQueryService` (`GetInvestigationAsync` and `GetReconciliationsAsync`) with explicit `t.TransactionType != TransactionType.Move` predicates so intra-warehouse moves always net to `0` (`Status = "Match"`, `Difference = 0`). | [`ReportsControllerTests.cs:111-263`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/ReportsControllerTests.cs#L111-L263) (`Wave14_GetInventoryInOutStock_AfterPutawayMove_ExcludesIntraWarehouseMoveFromInOutTotals`, `4/4 PASS`) |
| **`Phân vùng 1` — `Subagent 1A` (`3bfdfee4`)** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`BusinessPartnerService.cs:204-282`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Services/BusinessPartnerService.cs#L204-L282), [`UnitRepository.cs:66-109`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/UnitRepository.cs#L66-L109), [`ProductCatalogService.cs:680-750`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application/Services/ProductCatalogService.cs#L680-L750), [`ProductCatalogRepository.cs:256-306`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Infrastructure/Repositories/ProductCatalogRepository.cs#L256-L306) | **Master-Data Deletion Guards (`BusinessPartner`, `Unit`, `Product`) Against Dependent Transaction & AP/AR Tables:** Deleting a `BusinessPartner` or `Unit` that is already referenced by `PartnerDebtInvoices`, `PartnerDebtLedgers`, `PurchaseOrders`, `SalesOrders`, `ImportReceipts`, `ExportReceipts`, `PurchaseOrderLines`, `AsnLines`, `ImportReceiptDetails`, `PutawayTaskItems`, or `ReceivingObservationVersions` previously risked hitting raw SQL Server `FK_547` (`500 Internal Server Error`). **Fix:** Added pre-delete dependency checks across all 9 dependent partner tables in `BusinessPartnerService.DeleteAsync` and all 7 dependent UoM tables in `UnitRepository.DeleteAsync` (plus defensive `DbUpdateException` SQL `547` translation to `ConflictException` `409 Conflict` with clear Vietnamese domain messages), and verified `ProductCatalogService.DeleteProductAsync` / `ProductCatalogRepository.GetProductDeleteUsageBreakdownAsync`. | [`MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs:1252-1356`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Application.Tests/MultiTenantIsolationSqlTests.cs#L1252-L1356), [`MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs:1054-1195`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/ERP.Api.Tests/MultiTenantHttpAuthAndLifecycleTests.cs#L1054-L1195) |

| **`Phân vùng 2` — `Subagent 2A` (`992a8b99`)** | **HIGH** | `RESOLVED WITH EVIDENCE` | [`production-ui.css:357-395`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/ui/production-ui.css#L357-L395), [`PartnerDebts.tsx:222-370`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/pages/PartnerDebts.tsx#L222-L370) | **Fix Mid-Character Table Wrapping & Broken Currency Numbers on `/partner-debts` (`1440x900`):** Global `overflow-wrap: anywhere` on `th, td` in `production-ui.css` caused table headers (`CHỨN / G TỪ / KHO`), document codes (`AR-ER-QA0001`), currency amounts (`800.0 / 00 / VND`, `11.000 / .000 / VND`), and dates (`17/1 / 1/20 / 26`) to break mid-word across multiple lines. **Fix:** Removed `overflow-wrap: anywhere` from `th, td`; enforced `white-space: nowrap` on all `th`; added `.ui-cell-nowrap` (for codes, dates, statuses, actions), `.ui-cell-numeric` (`white-space: nowrap; text-align: right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` for currency/numbers), and `.ui-cell-partner` (`min-width: 200px; overflow-wrap: break-word; word-break: normal` for partner names); and applied them across all 3 tables in `PartnerDebts.tsx`. | [`PartnerDebtsAndScanner.test.tsx`](file:///d:/ERP_KHO/frontend/src/pages/PartnerDebtsAndScanner.test.tsx), Live Playwright Screenshot [`gate4-wave14-partner-debts-desktop.png`](file:///C:/Users/van40/.gemini/antigravity/brain/16f9bcc3-2924-4af8-9fa0-4b8b8bfc08bb/gate4-wave14-partner-debts-desktop.png) |
